Key components:
- Internal score function using FFT for any array length. Return maxcoeff, maybe active coefficients.
- Optimize sequence with multiplicative subgradient / mirror descent on Lp potential.
- Use coarse-to-fine: optimize n=128, 256, 512, 1024, 2048, 4096, 8192 with time allocation.
- Start from multiple diverse initializations at coarse: uniform, random positive, sinusoidal, polynomial, previous height_sequence_1 (resampled), maybe alternations.
- For each, run a limited number of iterations with adaptive step and p/λ annealing.
- Keep best per length and globally; refine best by interpolating to next length.

Need implement fast potential gradient.

Detailed score and gradient:

Internal functions:
```
def eval_fast(a, m=None):
    a=np.asarray(a,float)
    n=a.size
    if m is None: m=1
    while m < 2*n-1: m<<=1
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a,m)
    conv=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa,m)[:2*n-1]
    conv=np.maximum(conv,0)
    return conv, 2.0*n*conv.max()
```
But we need normalize to sum=1? Score scale invariant; for potential h = n*conv / S^2? Better keep a normalized sum=1. Then score=2*n*conv.max().

Softmax gradient function:
```
def softmax(z):
  z = z - z.max(); e=np.exp(z); return e/e.sum()
```

def multiplicative_refine(a, time_budget, p=8, lr_start=0.2):
  a=normalize(a)
  best=a.copy(); best_score=score_fast(a)
  it=0
  while time < deadline:
    conv, score=...
    h=n*conv
    # weights maybe p-power normalized
    x=np.clip(h/(1.1*max(h,1e-12)),0,1)
    w=x**p
    # maybe subtract small fraction top? Actually optimize minimize ∑w? Use gradient of ∑h^p: w=p h^{p-1}
    grad = corr(a,w)
    # scale: grad_mean = mean(grad); if zero break
    # Try line search lr in [lr*0.5, lr, lr*2]? To save, accept if improved else reduce.
    for trial_lr in (lr, lr*0.5, lr*0.25, lr*2):
       child = a*np.exp(-trial_lr*grad/ (grad.mean()+1e-300))
       child=np.maximum(child, 1e-12)
       child/=child.sum()
       cscore=score_fast(child)
       if cscore<best_score_global? We want local accept if <score. Keep best child.
    accept best child if improved, else break/reduce p? add noise.
```

`_lp_obj(z)` uses global.

```
def _softmax_from_z(z):
    z = np.asarray(z, dtype=np.float64)
    z = z - z.max()
    # if max not finite
    if not np.isfinite(z).all(): z=np.zeros_like(z)
    e=np.exp(z)
    s=e.sum()
    if s <=0 or not finite: return np.ones_like(z)/z.size
    return e/s

def _conv_sq_fft(a, m):
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a, m)
    c=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa, m)
    c[:2*a.size-1] = np.maximum(c[:2*a.size-1], 0.0)
    return c[:2*a.size-1]
```
Be careful: `fa*fa` not `fa*conj(fa)` because convolve with itself, not power spectrum. Yes, A^2 not |A|^2. Use `fa * fa` (complex square). For real a, circular padded. Good. If a complex? no. Use `fa**2`.
Correlation:
```
def _corr_a_w(a,w,m):
   A=np.fft.rfft(a,m)
   W=np.fft.rfft(w,m)
   c=np.fft.irfft(W*np.conj(A), m)
   return np.maximum(c[:a.size],0?)
```
Gradient may be negative due numeric; use real and maybe no clip? w,a nonnegative, result nonnegative; small negatives okay. Use `c = c[:n].real; c = np.where(c>0,c,0.0)` to ensure nonnegative for multiplicative; for L-BFGS gradient should be nonnegative anyway; clipping helps.
But derivative can be zero; no negatives.

Detailed inits:
```
def _make_inits(n, seed, best=None):
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    inits=[]
    def add(a): normalize; if finite and size n; append
    # uniform
    add(np.ones(n))
    # previous best
    if best is not None: add(resample(best,n))
    else ref
    # powers
    x=(np.arange(n)+0.5)/n
    for g in [-0.5,-0.25,0,0.25,0.5,0.75,1,1.5,2.5]:
       a=np.power(x+1e-12,g)
       # if negative g, endpoints large but finite with eps
       add(a); add(a[::-1])
    # exponential
    for g in [-3,-1,-0.5,0.5,1,3]:
       a=np.exp(g*(2*x-1)); add(a); add(a[::-1])
    # beta
    for al in [-0.5,0,0.25,0.5,1,2]:
       for be in [-0.5,0,0.25,0.5,1,2]:
           loga=al*np.log(np.maximum(x,1e-4))-be*np.log(np.maximum(1-x,1e-4))
           a=np.exp(loga-loga.max()); add(a); add(a[::-1])
    # sinusoidal
    for k in [1,2,3,4]:
       for phase in [0, np.pi/2]:
          a=1+0.8*np.sin(2*np.pi*k*x+phase); add(a);
          a=1+0.8*np.cos(...); add
    # shape with two humps: exp(-((x-c)/w)^2) sum? Use mixture gaussians:
    for c in [0.25,0.75]:
      w=0.2
      a=np.exp(-((x-c)/w)**2); add(a)
      # sum of two gaussians
    # random dirichlet
    for alpha in [0.5,1,2,5,10]:
       a=rng.dirichlet(np.full(n,alpha))
       add(a)
    # random smooth: Fourier random coefficients positive
    for _ in range(5):
       freqs = rng.integers(1, n//2, size=min(10,n//4))
       amps = rng.normal(0,1,size=len)
       phases = rng.uniform(0,2pi)
       a=1+np.sum(amps*np.cos(2πfreq*x+phases)*?); clip positive small
       add(a)
    # maybe row from previous ref if length differs
    # If too many, limit? We'll evaluate all for small n. For n large, maybe still okay? n=8192, number inits maybe ~100, FFT each maybe 0.1s? 100*8ms=0.8s okay.
    return inits
```
Need ensure no lambdas. Use loops.

def _refine_lbfgs(a, max_iters, p_schedule):
   n=a.size
   global _N,_M,_P,_HCLIP
   a=_normalize(a)
   _N=n; _M=_next_fft_len(n)
   best=a.copy(); best_score=_score_fast(a)
   # p schedule list e.g. [2,4,8,16]
   for p in p_schedule:
      if time.monotonic()>deadline? use global _DEADLINE? We can't access nested? Global variable set.
      # set H based on current best hmax
      hmax = _score_fast(best)/2  # since score=2hmax
      _P = int(p)
      _HCLIP = max(1e-6, hmax*1.25 + 1e-6)
      z0 = _initial_z(best)
      bounds = [(-45,45)]*n
      res = _scipy_minimize(_lp_obj, z0, jac=_lp_grad, method='L-BFGS-B', bounds=bounds, options={...})
      if res.x finite: a=softmax(res.x); a=normalize; if score<best: best=a
      else break
   return best,best_score
```

      w/=w.sum()
      grad=_corr_a_w(a,w,m)
      gmax=grad.max(); if gmax<=1e-300: break
      grad/=gmax
      # maybe use top exact? Also add small random log noise

def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    start=time.monotonic()
    deadline=start + max(30, float(budget_s)-10)
    try:
       # initial best from height_sequence_1 if exact?
       best=_normalize(_resample(height_sequence_1, choose length?)) maybe length 1024? But if length 4806, storing okay. Could keep as candidate for exact later.
       lengths = [64,128,256,512,1024,2048,4096,8192]
       # optional final length 4806? Use 8192.
       # For each length, get inits, top, refine.
       for n in lengths:
          if time > deadline - 120: break
          inits=_make_inits(n, seed, best)
          scored=... topK depending n
          # allocate time per candidate maybe (deadline - time - reserve)/total_tasks
          for a in topK:
             if time > deadline - 60: break
             rem = deadline - time - 30
             share=rem/max(1, remaining tasks)
             a2,s=_refine_sequence(a, budget=share, n=n)
             if s<best_score: best=a2
             # also if time left, multiplicative
          # After length, maybe exact best? maybe not.
       # final polish best at its length with multiplicative for remaining
       return exact_best_list
```

Correlation with `W*np.conj(A)`: For real, `ifft(W*conj(A))` = circular cross-correlation. Need verify with small example to ensure gradient direction. Suppose a=[1,0], w=[1,0]. corr_l=∑ a_j w_{l+j}. l=0: a0 w0=1. l=1: a0 w1? w1=0 =>0. Our FFT m≥? A=[1,0], W=[1,0]. ifft(W*conj(A)) = ifft([1,0]) = [1,0]. Good. Reverse? If w=[0,1], a=[1,0]. Expected l=0:w0? a0 w0=0; l=1: a0 w1=1. W=[0,1]. Product conj(A)*W=[0,1]; ifft -> [0,1]. Good. So correct.
Gradient factor: For c_k, derivative 2 a_{k-l}. If w_k weights on c, grad_l=2∑ w_k a_{k-l}. corr_l=∑_j a_j w_{l+j} with j=k-l => ∑ w_{l+j} a_j =∑ w_k a_{k-l}. Good.

```
import numpy as np
import time
try:
    from scipy.optimize import minimize as _minimize
except Exception:
    _minimize = None

_N = 0
_M = 0
_P = 2
_HCLIP = 1.0

def _propose_doc = "..."
def _normalize(...): ...
def _fft_len(n): ...
def _conv_sq(a,m): ...
def _corr(a,w,m): ...
def _fast_score(a): ...
def _fast_hmax(a): ...
...
def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    """Search for low self-convolution sequences using ..."""
    global ...
    start=...
    deadline=...
    best = None; best_score=inf
    # get reference
    ref=None
    try: ref=np.asarray(height_sequence_1,float); ref=clip...
    except: pass
    # maybe quick candidates at a medium length 1024 to set best early? 
    lengths=[128,256,512,1024,2048,4096,8192]
    # But if budget small, maybe skip high? Use deadline.
    for idx,n in enumerate(lengths):
       if time.monotonic() > deadline - 240: break
       if not (100 <= n <= 100000): continue
       # if time left estimate? okay
       inits=_make_inits(n, seed + idx*101, ref if ref is not None else best)
       scored=[]
       for a in inits:
          if time>deadline-300: break
          sc=_fast_score(a)
          if np.isfinite(sc): scored.append((sc,a))
       scored.sort()
       if not scored: continue
       top_count = 2 if n<=2048 else 2
       # ensure includes best ref if present
       cands=[scored[0][1]]
       if len(scored)>1: cands.append(scored[1][1])
       if best is not None:
          cands.append(normalize(resample(best,n)))
       # maybe scored[3]
       # Deduplicate by fingerprint? not.
       # time per candidate reserve
       for ci,c in enumerate(cands):
          if time > deadline - 180: break
          # If initial score already worse than best and no time? still optimize.
          # maxiter depend n
          if n<=256: maxit=300; p_sch=[2,4,8,16]
          elif n<=1024: maxit=200; p_sch=[4,8,16]
          else: maxit=100; p_sch=[8,16]
          a2,s2=_refine_lbfgs(c, maxit, p_sch, deadline)
          if s2 < best_score: best=a2; best_score=s2
          # multiplicative polish if time
          if time < deadline - 120:
             a3,s3=_refine_multiplicative(a2, deadline - 90)
             if s3 < best_score: best=a3; best_score=s3
    # final polish best with multiplicative
    if best is None: best=_normalize(np.ones(1024)); best_score=_fast_score(best)
    if time < deadline - 60:
        a4,s4=_refine_multiplicative(best, deadline - 60)
        if s4 < best_score: best=a4; best_score=s4
    # exact verification
    candidates=[best]
    if ref is not None: candidates.append(normalize(ref))
    # maybe last stage best? keep list stage_bests
    # Evaluate exact with time guard
    best_seq=candidates[0]; best_exact=np.inf
    for seq in candidates:
       if time>deadline-15: break
       try:
          sc=evaluate_sequence([float(x) for x in seq])
          if np.isfinite(sc) and sc<best_exact:
             best_exact=sc; best_seq=seq
       except Exception:
          pass
    return [float(x) for x in best_seq]
```

```
def _multiplicative(a, deadline, seed):
   a=normalize; best=a.copy(); best_score=fast_score(a)
   rng=default_rng(seed)
   p=8; H0=best_score/2*1.1
   eta=0.1; it=0
   while time<deadline:
      conv=...; h=n*conv; H=max(1e-9, 1.1*hmax)
      x=clip(h/H,0,1)
      w=P* x**(P-1)/H
      corr=...; grad=2*corr; dot=a.dot(grad); proj=grad-a*dot
      scale=max(abs(proj)); if scale<1e-12 break
      v=proj/scale
      # maybe anneal p: start p=4, increase to 16
      p = ...
      for trial in [eta, eta*0.5, eta*2]:
         child=a*np.exp(np.clip(-trial*v, -30,30))
         child=np.maximum(child,1e-12); child/=child.sum()
         sc=fast_score(child)
         if sc < best_score: best=child; best_score=sc; eta=min(1.0,eta*1.1); break
      else:
         eta=max(0.01, eta*0.7)
         if eta<0.02:
            # add noise
            v += rng.normal(0,0.05,size=n)
            scale=max(abs(v)); v/=scale; trial=0.05
            child=...
            if sc < best: accept eta=0.05
         else?
      # if time
```

Let's write helpers concretely.

```
def _as_float_array(x):
    try: arr=np.asarray(x,dtype=np.float64)
    except: return None
    if arr.size==0: return None
    arr=arr.ravel()
    arr=arr[np.isfinite(arr)]
    return np.maximum(arr,0.0)
```
For resample, need length.

```
def _normalize(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    a=np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a=np.maximum(a,0.0)
    cap = 1000.0 # no need
    s=a.sum()
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        return np.ones(a.size, dtype=np.float64)
    return a/s
```
If a has values >1000? Normalizing sum1 makes max maybe >1 if n=1; n>=? If n=1, score 2? But we use n≥128. Fine.

`_fast_score`:
```
def _fast_score(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,float)
    n=a.size
    if n==0: return np.inf
    s=a.sum()
    if s <=1e-12 or not finite: return np.inf
    a=a/s
    m=_next_fft_len(n)
    conv=_conv_sq_fft(a,m)
    if conv.size==0: return np.inf
    return float(2.0*n*max(conv))
```
`np.max` of conv; use `conv.max()`.

`_next_fft_len(n)`:
```
def _next_fft_len(n):
    need=2*n-1
    m=1
    while m<need: m <<=1
    return m
```
For n up to 16384, m=32768.

`_refine_lbfgs(a, maxiter, p_schedule, deadline)`:
```
global _N,_M,_P,_HCLIP
a=_normalize(a); n=a.size
if _minimize is None: return a,_fast_score(a)
_N=n; _M=_next_fft_len(n)
best=a.copy(); best_score=_fast_score(a)
z0=np.clip(_initial_z(best), -40,40)
bounds=[(-40.0,40.0)]*n
for p in p_schedule:

`_softmax(z)`:
```
def _softmax(z):
    z=np.asarray(z,float)
    if z.size==0: return np.array([])
    if not np.isfinite(z).all(): z=np.zeros(z.shape)
    z=z-z.max()
    e=np.exp(z)
    s=e.sum()
    if s<=0 or not finite: return np.ones_like(z)/z.size
    return e/s
```
If z all -inf? no.

```
def _refine_multiplicative(a, deadline, seed=1):
   a=_normalize(a); n=a.size; m=_next_fft_len(n); rng=np.random.default_rng(seed+n)
   best=a.copy(); best_score=_fast_score(a)
   eta=0.1; p=4; stall=0
   while time.monotonic() < deadline:
      conv=_conv_sq_fft(a,m); score=2*n*conv.max(); h=n*conv; hmax=h.max()
      if score < best_score: best=a; best_score=score
      H=max(1e-9, 1.1*hmax)
      x=np.clip(h/H,0,1)
      w=p * np.power(x, p-1)/H
      corr=_correlate(a,w,m)
      grad=2*corr
      dot=float(np.dot(a,grad))
      proj=grad - a*dot
      scale=float(np.max(np.abs(proj)))
      if not finite or scale<1e-14: break
      v=proj/scale
      # Add tiny noise to avoid exact saddle
      noise=1e-4 if stall>5 else 0
      if noise: v=v + rng.normal(0,noise, size=n); scale2=max(np.max(abs(v)),1e-12); v/=scale2
      # Try step sizes
      improved=False
      for trial in (eta, 0.5*eta):
         logfac=np.exp(np.clip(-trial*v, -50,50))
         child=a*logfac
         child=np.maximum(child,1e-12)
         child/=child.sum()
         csc=_fast_score(child)
         if np.isfinite(csc) and csc < best_score:
            a=child; best=child; best_score=csc; improved=True
            eta=min(1.0, eta*1.1)
            stall=0
            break
      if not improved:
         eta=max(0.01, eta*0.6); stall+=1
         if eta<0.015:
            reset eta=0.1; p=min(16,int(p*1.5)); # increase focus
            # random perturbation
            child=a*np.exp(rng.normal(0,0.05,size=n)); normalize; if score<best accept else keep a? If not, continue
      # also maybe increase p slowly
      if stall>10: p=min(24, p+2)
   return best,best_score
```

def _ls_obj(z):
  a=softmax; conv=...; h=n*conv
  u=_LAM*(h-_REF)
  u_max=np.max(u)

def _ls_grad(z):

  corr=_correlate(a,s,m)
  grad=2*corr
  dot=a.dot(grad)
  return grad - a*dot
```

Multiplicative refine with logsumexp:
```
def _refine_multiplicative(a, deadline, seed=123):
   a=normalize; best=a.copy(); best_score=fast_score(a)
   n=a.size; m=_next_fft_len(n); rng=default_rng(seed)
   lam=5.0; eta=0.1; stall=0
   while time < deadline:
      conv=...; score=...; h=n*conv
      if score<best: update
      ref=max(1e-9, 1.02*max(h))
      u=lam*(h-ref); umax=max; v=u-umax; e=exp(v); s=e/e.sum()
      corr=_correlate(a,s,m)
      grad=2*n*corr
      dot=a.dot(grad)
      proj=grad-a*dot
      scale=maxabs(proj)
      if scale <1e-12: break
      v=proj/scale
      if stall>5: v += rng.normal(0,0.01,size=n); scale=maxabs(v); v/=scale
      improved=False
      for trial in (eta, 0.5*eta, 2*eta):
         child=a*np.exp(np.clip(-trial*v,-50,50)); child=clip; child/=sum
         csc=fast_score(child)
         if csc<best_score: accept; break
      if not improved: eta=max(0.005, eta*0.7); stall+=1; if eta<0.01: lam = min(100, lam*1.8); eta=0.1
      else: eta=min(1.0,eta*1.05); stall=0; if score<best_score*0.99: lam=min(120,lam*1.1)
   return best, best_score
```
Because objective gradient projected may be large at high n; scale normalizes. Step eta relative to projected gradient scale; maybe 0.05 good.
We can make `eta` start 0.05 for high n? Use 0.1.

`_resample(seq,n)`:
```
def _resample(seq, n):
    arr=np.asarray(seq,float).ravel()
    arr=arr[np.isfinite(arr)]
    arr=np.maximum(arr,0.0)
    s=arr.sum()
    if arr.size==0 or s<=0:
       return np.ones(n)
    if n==1:
       return np.ones(1)
    xs=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,arr.size)
    xn=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
    y=np.interp(xn,xs, arr/s)
    return _normalize(y)
```

`_try_add`:
```
def _try_add(out, a, n):
    a=np.asarray(a,float).ravel()
    if a.size != n:
       if a.size==0: return
       a=_resample(a,n)
    a=_normalize(a)
    if a.size == n and np.all(np.isfinite(a)) and a.sum()>0:
       out.append(a)
```

Now, `_score_inits`:
```
def _score_inits(inits, deadline, keep=5):
   scored=[]
   for i,a in enumerate(inits):
      if time.monotonic()>deadline-30: break
      sc=_fast_score(a)
      if np.isfinite(sc): scored.append((sc,i,a))
   scored.sort()
   res=[]
   for item in scored:
      res.append(item[2])
      if len(res)>=keep: break
   return res
```
No lambda. If scores tied, sort by i then a? Tuple (score,i,a); if score equal compare i, not a. Good.

`_score_inits`:
```
def _score_inits(inits, deadline, keep):
    scored=[]
    for i,a in enumerate(inits):
       if time.monotonic() > deadline - 20:
           break
       sc=_fast_score(a)
       if np.isfinite(sc):
          scored.append((float(sc), i, a))
    scored.sort()
    res=[]
    for it in scored:
       res.append(it[2])
       if len(res) >= keep:
          break
    return res
```

    ncols = min(20, max(3, n//50))
    freqs = rng.integers(1, max(2, n//2), size=ncols)
    amps = rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, size=ncols)
    phases = rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0*np.pi, size=ncols)
    comp = np.zeros(n)
    for ii in range(ncols):
        comp = comp + amps[ii]*np.cos(2.0*np.pi*float(freqs[ii])*x + phases[ii])
    a = 1.0 + 0.5*np.tanh(comp)
    _try_add(out, a, n)
```

Now, `_refine_lbfgs`:
```
def _refine_lbfgs(init, maxiter, lam_schedule, deadline):
    global _N,_M,_LAM,_REF
    a=_normalize(init); n=a.size
    if n ==0: return a,np.inf
    if _minimize is None:
       return a,_fast_score(a)
    _N=n; _M=_next_fft_len(n)
    best=a.copy(); best_score=_fast_score(a)
    z0=np.clip(_initial_z(best), -45.0,45.0)
    bounds=[(-45.0,45.0)]*n
    for lam in lam_schedule:
       if time.monotonic() > deadline - 90:
           break
       hmax=best_score/2.0
       if not np.isfinite(hmax) or hmax <= 0:
           hmax=1.0
       _LAM=float(lam)
       _REF=max(1e-9, 1.02*hmax + 1e-6)
       # reduce maxiter if time little
       iters = max(5, int(min(maxiter, 1000)))
       options={'maxiter': iters, 'ftol':1e-12, 'gtol':1e-7, 'maxcor':20, 'maxls':10, 'disp':False}
       try:
          res=_minimize(_ls_obj, z0, jac=_ls_grad, method='L-BFGS-B', bounds=bounds, options=options)
          if res.x is not None:
             cand=_softmax_from_z(np.asarray(res.x, dtype=float))
             cand=_normalize(cand)
             sc=_fast_score(cand)
             if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
                best=cand; best_score=sc; z0=np.clip(_initial_z(best), -45.0,45.0)
       except Exception:
          pass
    return best,best_score
```
This may call minimize with same z0 for each λ; after improvement reset z0. If lambda increases, previous z still good.
Should we add a small randomization to z0 before each λ? Could. `z0 = np.clip(z0 + np.random.default_rng(...).normal(0,0.01), -45,45)` if no improvement? Not necessary.
Maybe if objective improved but not best? `minimize` always returns some x; even if score not lower, we could keep res.x as new z0 for next λ because L-BFGS may move along potential. But if it worsened exact score, maybe still good for next λ? We can set z0 from res.x if finite. But if it worsened, maybe bad. We can set z0 to res.x if objective lower than initial? `minimize` success? Hard. Keep only improved z0; else retain.

Now, `_refine_multiplicative`:
```
def _refine_multiplicative(init, deadline, seed=1):
    a=_normalize(init); n=a.size
    if n==0: return a,np.inf
    m=_next_fft_len(n)
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed+n)
    best=a.copy(); best_score=_fast_score(a)
    lam=5.0; eta=0.1; stall=0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        conv=_conv_sq_fft(a,m)
        score=float(2*n*max(0.0, conv.max()))
        if score < best_score:
            best=a.copy(); best_score=score
        h=n*conv
        hmax=float(h.max())
        if hmax <= 0: break
        ref=max(1e-9, 1.02*hmax+1e-6)
        u=lam*(h-ref)
        umax=float(np.max(u))
        v=u-umax
        e=np.exp(v)
        sw=float(np.sum(e))
        if sw <=0 or not finite: break
        s=e/sw
        corr=_correlate_fft(a,s,m)
        grad=2.0*n*corr
        dot=float(np.dot(a,grad))
        proj=grad - a*dot
        scale=float(np.max(np.abs(proj)))
        if not finite or scale < 1e-12:
            break
        zdir=proj/scale
        if stall > 4:
            zdir = zdir + rng.normal(0.0, 0.02, size=n)
            sc2=float(np.max(np.abs(zdir)))
            if sc2>0: zdir/=sc2
        improved=False
        for trial in (eta, 0.5*eta, 2.0*eta):
            child=a*np.exp(np.clip(-trial*zdir, -50.0, 50.0))
            child=np.maximum(child, 1e-12)
            csum=child.sum()
            if csum <=0: continue
            child=child/csum
            csc=_fast_score(child)
            if np.isfinite(csc) and csc < best_score:
                a=child; best=child; best_score=csc; improved=True
                eta=min(1.0, eta*1.1)
                stall=0
                # focus more if significant
                if best_score < score*0.99:
                    lam=min(120.0, lam*1.15)
                break
        if not improved:
            eta=max(0.01, eta*0.6)
            stall += 1
            if eta < 0.015:
                lam = min(150.0, max(2.0, lam*1.7))
                eta=0.1
        # optional increase lam slowly
        if stall == 0 and (int(lam*10)%10 == 0):
            pass
    return best,best_score
```

elif n <=4096: per=0.15
else: per=0.3
iters = int(max(5, min(maxiter, time_budget / (per*10)))) # assume 10 evals per iter
```
This caps. Good. Add in `_refine_lbfgs`.
Similarly, in main, before candidate, if time low, break. This prevents overrun.
Let's set per_iter times conservative:
- n<=128:0.005
- <=512:0.02
- <=2048:0.05
- <=4096:0.1
- else:0.2
Times multiplied by 15 for line search. So allowed iters = (remaining-60)/(per*15). For n=8192, per0.2*15=3s per iter, 1000s ->333, but maxiter80. For n=128, per0.005*15=0.075, 1000s ->13333, max 250. Good. Use `min(maxiter, allowed)`.
This is safer.

In `_refine_multiplicative`, loop checks deadline each iter. Each iter maybe 3 FFT; okay. Could run to deadline exactly.

Now, L-BFGS uses bounds list; if n large and maxiter small, okay.

Now, potential issue: `_ls_obj` and `_ls_grad` use global `_M`; if `_N` n and `_M` set. Good.

Let's now write final code elegantly.

One more thought: The objective's `sum_a` in evaluator is sum after clipping each x to max 1000 and min0. If sum=1, no issue. But if we return a numpy float32? We cast float. Good.

Now, final code block. Ensure no hidden unicode.

Let's produce final.
</think>

```python
"""
Search for a nonnegative sequence minimizing
    2*n*max(a convolve a) / (sum a)^2.

The method uses a coarse-to-fine optimization pipeline:
  * scale a is normalized to sum 1, so the objective becomes 2*n*max(a*a)
  * multiple smooth/non-smooth templates are generated at each length
  * the best templates are polished by a log-softmax surrogate for the
    maximum convolution coefficient, optimized with L-BFGS-B when scipy is
    available
  * a projected multiplicative mirror-descent polish is applied afterward
  * the best array is verified with the supplied exact evaluator before
    returning.
"""

import numpy as np
import time

try:
    from scipy.optimize import minimize as _scipy_minimize
except Exception:
    _scipy_minimize = None

# Globals used by the log-softmax objective / gradient supplied to scipy.
_N = 0
_M = 0
_LAM = 1.0
_REF = 1.0


def _normalize(a):
    """Return a finite nonnegative 1-D array of shape like a with sum 1."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    a = np.minimum(a, 1000.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        return np.full(a.size, 1.0 / float(a.size), dtype=np.float64)
    return a / s


def _next_fft_len(n):
    """Smallest power of two at least 2*n-1."""
    need = max(1, 2 * int(n) - 1)
    m = 1
    while m < need:
        m <<= 1
    return m


def _conv_sq_fft(a, m):
    """Full linear convolution a*a using a zero-padded FFT."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, m)
    c = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, m)
    c = c[:2 * n - 1]
    return np.maximum(c, 0.0)


def _correlate_fft(a, w, m):
    """
    First n entries of the cross-correlation
        out[l] = sum_j a[j] * w[l+j]
    for nonnegative real arrays, using zero-padding.
    """
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    w = np.asarray(w, dtype=np.float64)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, m)
    W = np.fft.rfft(w, m)
    c = np.fft.irfft(W * np.conj(A), m)
    c = c[:a.size].real
    return np.maximum(c, 0.0)


def _fast_score_m(a, m):
    """Internal fast score assuming a is normalized to sum 1."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.inf
    conv = _conv_sq_fft(a, m)
    if conv.size == 0:
        return np.inf
    return float(2.0 * n * conv.max())


def _fast_score(a):
    """Internal fast score for arbitrary nonnegative array."""
    a = _normalize(a)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.inf
    m = _next_fft_len(n)
    return _fast_score_m(a, m)


def _softmax_from_z(z):
    """Numerically stable softmax."""
    z = np.asarray(z, dtype=np.float64)
    if z.size == 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(z)):
        z = np.zeros(z.shape, dtype=np.float64)
    z = z - float(np.max(z))
    e = np.exp(z)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return np.full(z.size, 1.0 / float(z.size), dtype=np.float64)
    return e / s


def _initial_z(a):
    """Initial log-softmax parameter with mean zero, clipped for stability."""
    a = _normalize(a)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    floor = max(1e-12, 1e-12 / float(n))
    z = np.log(np.maximum(a, floor))
    z = z - float(np.mean(z))
    return np.clip(z, -45.0, 45.0)


def _ls_obj(z):
    """
    Smooth log-softmax objective:
        (1/lambda) log sum_k exp(lambda * (n*(a*a)_k - ref))
    This approximates max_k n*(a*a)_k.
    """
    a = _softmax_from_z(z)
    n = int(_N)
    m = int(_M)
    if n <= 0 or m <= 0:
        return 0.0
    conv = _conv_sq_fft(a, m)
    h = n * conv
    u = _LAM * (h - _REF)
    umax = float(np.max(u))
    v = u - umax
    e = np.exp(v)
    ssum = float(np.sum(e))
    if not np.isfinite(ssum) or ssum <= 0.0:
        return float(umax / _LAM)
    return float((umax + np.log(ssum)) / _LAM)


def _ls_grad(z):
    """Gradient of _ls_obj with respect to z = log a (softmax parameter)."""
    a = _softmax_from_z(z)
    n = int(_N)
    m = int(_M)
    if n <= 0 or m <= 0:
        return np.zeros_like(a)
    conv = _conv_sq_fft(a, m)
    h = n * conv
    u = _LAM * (h - _REF)
    umax = float(np.max(u))
    v = u - umax
    e = np.exp(v)
    ssum = float(np.sum(e))
    if not np.isfinite(ssum) or ssum <= 0.0:
        return np.zeros_like(a)
    s = e / ssum

    # d_u/da = n * 2 * correlation(a, weights)
    corr = _correlate_fft(a, s, m)
    grad_a = 2.0 * float(n) * corr

    # Project gradient onto the tangent space of the softmax simplex.
    dot = float(np.dot(a, grad_a))
    proj = grad_a - a * dot
    return np.where(np.isfinite(proj), proj, 0.0)


def _resample(seq, n):
    """Linearly resample a sequence to length n and normalize to sum 1."""
    arr = np.asarray(seq, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if arr.size == 0:
        return np.full(n, 1.0 / float(max(1, n)), dtype=np.float64)
    arr = arr[np.isfinite(arr)]
    arr = np.maximum(arr, 0.0)
    s = float(arr.sum())
    if arr.size == 0 or s <= 0.0:
        return np.full(n, 1.0 / float(max(1, n)), dtype=np.float64)
    if arr.size == 1:
        return np.full(n, 1.0 / float(max(1, n)), dtype=np.float64)
    xs = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, arr.size, dtype=np.float64)
    xn = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)
    y = np.interp(xn, xs, arr / s)
    return _normalize(y)


def _try_add(out, a, n):
    """Append a normalized nonnegative array of length n."""
    arr = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if arr.size != int(n):
        arr = _resample(arr, n)
    arr = _normalize(arr)
    if arr.size == int(n) and np.all(np.isfinite(arr)):
        out.append(arr)


def _make_inits(n, seed, best_seq=None):
    """Generate a diverse set of initial sequences for length n."""
    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed))
    out = []
    n = int(n)

    _try_add(out, np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64), n)

    try:
        ref = height_sequence_1  # noqa: F821
        _try_add(out, _resample(ref, n), n)
    except Exception:
        pass

    if best_seq is not None:
        _try_add(out, _resample(best_seq, n), n)

    x = (np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) + 0.5) / float(max(1, n))
    t = 2.0 * x - 1.0

    # Powers and exponentials, both monotone and reversed.
    for g in (-0.5, -0.25, 0.0, 0.25, 0.5, 0.75, 1.0, 1.5, 2.5):
        a = np.power(x + 1e-8, float(g))
        _try_add(out, a, n)
        _try_add(out, a[::-1], n)

    for g in (-3.0, -1.0, -0.5, 0.5, 1.0, 3.0):
        a = np.exp(float(g) * t)
        _try_add(out, a, n)
        _try_add(out, a[::-1], n)

    # Beta-like shapes, including mildly singular endpoints.
    lx = np.log(np.maximum(x, 1e-8))
    rx = np.log(np.maximum(1.0 - x, 1e-8))
    for al in (-0.5, 0.0, 0.25, 0.5, 1.0, 2.0):
        for be in (-0.5, 0.0, 0.25, 0.5, 1.0, 2.0):
            la = float(al) * lx - float(be) * rx
            la = la - float(np.max(la))
            a = np.exp(la)
            _try_add(out, a, n)
            _try_add(out, a[::-1], n)

    # Low-degree Legendre-like polynomials in the centered coordinate t.
    for c0 in (0.25, 0.5, 1.0):
        for c1 in (-1.0, -0.5, 0.0, 0.5, 1.0):
            for c2 in (-1.0, 0.0, 1.0):
                a = c0 + c1 * t + c2 * t * t
                _try_add(out, a, n)
                _try_add(out, a[::-1], n)
                for c3 in (-1.0, 0.0, 1.0):
                    b = a + c3 * t * t * t
                    _try_add(out, b, n)

    # Sinusoidal templates.
    for k in (1, 2, 3, 4, 6):
        for amp in (0.4, 0.7, 0.9):
            a = 1.0 + float(amp) * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * float(k) * x)
            _try_add(out, a, n)
            a = 1.0 + float(amp) * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * float(k) * x)
            _try_add(out, a, n)
            a = 0.5 + 0.5 * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * float(k) * x)
            _try_add(out, a, n)

    fourier_count = 8 if n <= 2048 else 4
    for _ in range(fourier_count):
        ncols = min(20, max(3, n // 50))
        fmax = max(2, n // 2)
        freqs = rng.integers(1, fmax, size=ncols)
        amps = rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, size=ncols)
        phases = rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi, size=ncols)
        comp = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        for ii in range(ncols):
            comp += float(amps[ii]) * np.cos(
                2.0 * np.pi * float(freqs[ii]) * x + float(phases[ii])
            )
        a = 1.0 + 0.5 * np.tanh(comp)
        _try_add(out, a, n)

    # Periodic weights times a power-law envelope.
    period_reps = 4 if n <= 2048 else 2
    for m in (2, 3, 4, 5, 8):
        for _ in range(period_reps):
            w = rng.dirichlet(np.ones(m, dtype=np.float64))
            reps = (n + m - 1) // m + 1
            base = np.tile(w, reps)[:n]
            epow = rng.uniform(-1.0, 2.0)
            env = np.power(x + 1e-8, float(epow))
            _try_add(out, base * env, n)
            _try_add(out, base[::-1] * env, n)

    # Pure random Dirichlet starts.
    dirichlet_alphas = (0.5, 1.0, 2.0, 5.0) if n <= 2048 else (1.0, 2.0)
    for alpha in dirichlet_alphas:
        a = rng.dirichlet(np.full(n, float(alpha), dtype=np.float64))
        _try_add(out, a, n)

    if len(out) == 0:
        _try_add(out, np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64), n)
    else:
        _try_add(out, np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64), n)

    return out


def _score_inits(inits, deadline, keep):
    """Score initial arrays and return the best `keep` arrays."""
    scored = []
    for i, a in enumerate(inits):
        if time.monotonic() > max(time.monotonic(), deadline - 20.0):
            break
        sc = _fast_score(a)
        if np.isfinite(sc):
            scored.append((float(sc), i, a))
    scored.sort()
    res = []
    for item in scored:
        res.append(item[2])
        if len(res) >= int(keep):
            break
    return res


def _refine_lbfgs(init, maxiter, lam_schedule, deadline):
    """Polish an array using log-softmax L-BFGS-B, if available."""
    global _N, _M, _LAM, _REF

    a = _normalize(init)
    n = int(a.size)
    if n == 0:
        return a, np.inf

    if _scipy_minimize is None:
        return a, _fast_score(a)

    m = _next_fft_len(n)
    _N = n
    _M = m

    best = a.copy()
    best_score = _fast_score_m(best, m)
    z0 = _initial_z(best)
    bounds = [(-45.0, 45.0)] * n

    for lam in lam_schedule:
        now = time.monotonic()
        if now > deadline - 80.0:
            break

        hmax = best_score / 2.0
        if not np.isfinite(hmax) or hmax <= 0.0:
            hmax = 1.0

        _LAM = float(lam)
        _REF = max(1e-9, 1.02 * hmax + 1e-6)

        # Rough time-based cap to avoid overrunning the hard deadline.
        if n <= 128:
            per_iter = 0.005
        elif n <= 512:
            per_iter = 0.015
        elif n <= 2048:
            per_iter = 0.05
        elif n <= 4096:
            per_iter = 0.12
        else:
            per_iter = 0.25

        remaining = max(1.0, float(deadline - time.monotonic()) - 60.0)
        allowed = int(max(5, remaining / max(1e-6, per_iter * 15.0)))
        iters = int(max(5, min(int(maxiter), allowed)))

        options = {
            "maxiter": iters,
            "ftol": 1e-12,
            "gtol": 1e-7,
            "maxcor": 20,
            "maxls": 10,
            "disp": False,
        }

        try:
            res = _scipy_minimize(
                _ls_obj,
                z0,
                jac=_ls_grad,
                method="L-BFGS-B",
                bounds=bounds,
                options=options,
            )
            if res.x is not None:
                cand = _softmax_from_z(np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64))
                cand = _normalize(cand)
                sc = _fast_score_m(cand, m)
                if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
                    best = cand.copy()
                    best_score = float(sc)
                    z0 = _initial_z(best)
        except Exception:
            pass

    return best, float(best_score)


def _refine_multiplicative(init, deadline, seed=1234):
    """
    Projected multiplicative mirror-descent polish using the same
    log-softmax surrogate.  Only moves that lower the internal fast score
    are accepted.
    """
    a = _normalize(init)
    n = int(a.size)
    if n == 0:
        return a, np.inf

    m = _next_fft_len(n)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed) + n)

    best = a.copy()
    best_score = _fast_score_m(best, m)

    lam = 5.0
    eta = 0.10
    stall = 0

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        conv = _conv_sq_fft(a, m)
        score = float(2.0 * n * conv.max())
        if score < best_score:
            best = a.copy()
            best_score = score

        h = n * conv
        hmax = float(h.max())
        if not np.isfinite(hmax) or hmax <= 0.0:
            break

        ref = max(1e-9, 1.02 * hmax + 1e-6)
        u = lam * (h - ref)
        umax = float(np.max(u))
        v = u - umax
        e = np.exp(v)
        ssum = float(np.sum(e))
        if not np.isfinite(ssum) or ssum <= 0.0:
            break
        s = e / ssum

        corr = _correlate_fft(a, s, m)
        grad = 2.0 * float(n) * corr
        dot = float(np.dot(a, grad))
        proj = grad - a * dot
        proj = np.where(np.isfinite(proj), proj, 0.0)

        scale = float(np.max(np.abs(proj)))
        if not np.isfinite(scale) or scale <= 1e-14:
            break

        zdir = proj / scale

        if stall > 4:
            zdir = zdir + rng.normal(0.0, 0.02, size=n)
            sc2 = float(np.max(np.abs(zdir)))
            if sc2 > 1e-30:
                zdir = zdir / sc2

        improved = False
        for trial in (eta, 0.5 * eta, 2.0 * eta):
            child = a * np.exp(np.clip(-trial * zdir, -50.0, 50.0))
            child = np.maximum(child, 1e-12)
            csum = float(child.sum())
            if not np.isfinite(csum) or csum <= 0.0:
                continue
            child = child / csum
            csc = _fast_score_m(child, m)
            if np.isfinite(csc) and csc < best_score:
                a = child
                best = child.copy()
                best_score = float(csc)
                improved = True
                eta = min(1.0, eta * 1.08)
                stall = 0
                if best_score < score * 0.995:
                    lam = min(120.0, lam * 1.15)
                break

        if not improved:
            eta = max(0.01, eta * 0.6)
            stall += 1
            if eta < 0.015:
                lam = min(150.0, max(2.0, lam * 1.7))
                eta = 0.10

    return best, float(best_score)


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    """
    Search for the best sequence and return it as a Python list of floats.
    """
    try:
        start = time.monotonic()
        total_budget = float(budget_s)
        if not np.isfinite(total_budget) or total_budget <= 0.0:
            total_budget = 2400.0
        deadline = start + max(60.0, total_budget - 20.0)

        best = None
        best_score = np.inf
        stage_bests = []

        lengths = [128, 256, 512, 1024, 2048, 4096, 8192]

        for n in lengths:
            if time.monotonic() > deadline - 300.0:
                break

            n = int(n)
            inits = _make_inits(n, int(seed) + n, best)
            top = _score_inits(inits, deadline, keep=3)

            if not top:
                continue

            cands = []
            for a in top:
                cands.append(a)

            if best is not None:
                rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed) + 1000003 * n)
                nb = _resample(best, n)
                if nb.size == n:
                    nb = nb * np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.10, size=n))
                    nb = _normalize(nb)
                    cands.append(nb)

            # Add one mid-priced initializer for extra diversity.
            if len(inits) > 40:
                cands.append(inits[len(inits) // 2])

            if n <= 128:
                maxit = 260
                lams = [1.0, 3.0, 10.0, 30.0, 80.0]
            elif n <= 512:
                maxit = 180
                lams = [1.0, 5.0, 20.0, 60.0]
            elif n <= 2048:
                maxit = 120
                lams = [3.0, 10.0, 30.0, 80.0]
            else:
                maxit = 80
                lams = [5.0, 20.0, 60.0]

            for idx, c in enumerate(cands):
                if time.monotonic() > deadline - 240.0:
                    break

                a2, s2 = _refine_lbfgs(c, maxit, lams, deadline)
                if np.isfinite(s2) and s2 < best_score:
                    best = a2.copy()
                    best_score = float(s2)
                    stage_bests.append(a2.copy())
                    if len(stage_bests) > 10:
                        stage_bests.pop(0)

                if time.monotonic() < deadline - 240.0:
                    a3, s3 = _refine_multiplicative(
                        a2,
                        deadline - 240.0,
                        seed=int(seed) + 17 * n + idx,
                    )
                    if np.isfinite(s3) and s3 < best_score:
                        best = a3.copy()
                        best_score = float(s3)
                        stage_bests.append(a3.copy())
                        if len(stage_bests) > 10:
                            stage_bests.pop(0)

            print(
                f"n={n} internal_best={best_score:.9f} "
                f"elapsed={time.monotonic()-start:.1f}s",
                flush=True,
            )

        if best is None or not np.isfinite(best_score):
            best = _normalize(np.ones(1024, dtype=np.float64))
            best_score = _fast_score(best)

        # Final polish of the best array.
        if time.monotonic() < deadline - 60.0:
            a4, s4 = _refine_multiplicative(
                best,
                deadline - 60.0,
                seed=int(seed) + 999983,
            )
            if np.isfinite(s4) and s4 < best_score:
                best = a4
                best_score = float(s4)

        # Exact verification.
        final_candidates = []
        if best is not None:
            final_candidates.append(best)
        for arr in stage_bests[-5:]:
            final_candidates.append(arr)

        try:
            ref = height_sequence_1  # noqa: F821
            refn = _normalize(ref)
            if refn.size > 0 and refn.size <= 20000:
                final_candidates.append(refn)
        except Exception:
            pass

        best_seq = _normalize(best)
        best_exact = np.inf
        for arr in final_candidates:
            if time.monotonic() > deadline - 25.0:
                break
            arr = _normalize(arr)
            if arr.size == 0 or arr.size > 20000:
                continue
            seq_list = [float(v) for v in arr]
            try:
                sc = evaluate_sequence(seq_list)  # noqa: F821
                sc = float(sc)
            except Exception:
                sc = np.inf
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_exact:
                best_exact = sc
                best_seq = arr

        if not np.isfinite(best_exact):
            best_seq = _normalize(best)

        out = []
        for v in best_seq:
            vf = float(v)
            if not np.isfinite(vf) or vf < 0.0:
                vf = 0.0
            if vf > 1000.0:
                vf = 1000.0
            out.append(vf)

        return out

    except Exception:
        try:
            return [float(v) for v in _normalize(height_sequence_1)]  # noqa: F821
        except Exception:
            return [1.0]
```
